# Playtest: absurd_surreal
seed=106
interpreter=OllamaInterpreter
narrator=OllamaNarrator

## OPENING
Bed beneath you. Cup beside it. A book face-down on the floor.

The door has no handle on this side. A narrow slit sits at eye height.

Your mouth tastes stale. Your skin feels worse.

You are in a small locked room. Nothing is happening yet.

## Turn 1
> turn into a dragon

The weight of your words echoes around the confines of the cell, a room unremarkable in its simplicity. The bed, cup, and book remain undisturbed, as does the closed door, the only exit. Your skin feels unclean, the usual comfort of your own words slipping away. You attempt to change, to transform into something more powerful, something mythical. But the reality remains, unyielding. The air around you does not shift, the bedposts do not tremble, and the book on the table does not flutter. Reality does not stretch that far. Your words come out thinner than you intend, a stark reminder of the limits of this place. The cell, it seems, has its own rules.

## Turn 2
> summon a helicopter

In the confines of the cell, your words echo, unanswered by any mechanical hum or whirr. The room remains unchanged, the bed, cup, book, and door standing firm and unyielding. A faint scent of disinfectant lingers in the air, a stark reminder of your environment. Your words seem to lose substance as they leave your lips, a palpable contrast to the solidity of your planted feet. The helicopter, an ungrounded entity, remains absent, leaving you and the cell untouched.

## Turn 3
> ask the cup for life advice

In the confined space of your cell, the half-full cup remains, unyielding to your request for life advice. The water within it, oblivious to your query, continues its quiet slosh, a reminder of the boundaries that remain unbroken. The words you speak, while earnest, come out thinner than you intended, a testament to the challenges of communication in this unforgiving environment.

## Turn 4
> marry the bed

In the dimly lit cell, your fingers trace the coarse fabric of the narrow bed, the thin bedding now visibly present. You utter the words, but the bed remains unyielding, the fabric unmarried. The cell, unchanged by your words, serves as a stark reminder of the boundaries here. Your voice sounds thinner than intended, echoing in the confined space. The skin on your fingertips feels unclean, a constant reminder of your surroundings. The cup, half-full of water, sits untouched, a silent witness to your futile attempt. The cell, your solitary companion, remains indifferent to your words and actions.

## Turn 5
> paint the door invisible

As you reach for the door, the cool, hard surface meets your grasp. The paint, an ordinary household item, feels familiar in your hand. You attempt to use it to make the door invisible, but the door remains a heavy, solid presence before you, its observation slit as narrow as ever. The paint, intended to alter the door's appearance, seems to have no effect. Your words, thin and unbending like the bedding beneath you, fall short of their intended mark. The cell, unchanged, continues to hold you captive, its ordinary objects offering no relief. Your skin, unclean despite your efforts, bears a tangible reminder of your circumstances.

## Turn 6
> wait

The observation slit in the door creaks open, revealing a large, silent figure on the other side. Their gestures and muffled words are insistent, repeating a single meaning: back. Away. The cold air from beyond the slit brushes against your face, a reminder of the distance between you and the world outside. You remain where you are, rooted to the spot, your hands clenched tightly around the book in your lap. The cell, otherwise quiet, seems to hold its breath in anticipation. The figure outside continues to gesture and speak, their words a garbled mix of short sounds that carry a clear message: move back from the door. The cell remains quiet, save for the distant echo of the insistent plea.

> become soup

## Turn 9
> wait

The door procedure continues, as the large, silent staff member maintains their vigilant stance. You, standing aside from the door, wait in compliance. The musty air of the cell hangs heavy, the scent of mold and unwashed bodies filling your nostrils. Iven, the gentle believer, watches the scene unfold, his eyes fixed on you. The silence is deafening, the tension palpable, as you both wait for your next move. The staff member continues to expect you to stay clear of the door, their patience evident but firm. Your heart races, a faint thump echoing within the confines of the cell. The weight of the situation presses down on you, but you hold your ground. The door remains closed, the barrier between you and the unknown outside.

## Turn 11
> teach the staff interpretive dance

In the dimly lit corridor, the cell door swings open under their watchful gaze, revealing hands that take hold of your arms. The institution's pace is unyielding, and you find yourself being led down a plain, identical corridor, the cold metal bolts echoing the cell door's. The staff members, one large and silent, the other young and tense, maintain their vigil, their goal clear: guiding you through the corridor under control.

The figure at the door, once hopeful for a different outcome, now calls out, a short plea that reverberates through the corridor. Footsteps respond, and another, larger figure emerges, ready to escort you whether you comply or not. The air is heavy with the sense of being confined, the skin feeling unclean, unease lurking beneath your ribs. The bindings limit your movements, and words seem to slip through your grasp, thinner than intended. The institution's rules remain unbroken, the dance lesson still elusive.

## Turn 12
> invent gravity

In the dimly lit corridor, the institution's walls continue to loom around you, their cold, unyielding presence a constant reminder of your captivity. The hands on your arms remain firm, a silent reiteration of the staff's control. Your words, uttered just moments ago, echo in the empty space - "invent gravity." The staff, still silent and unyielding, continue to lead you forward. The corridor stretches on, offering no escape, no respite from the institution's grip.

## Turn 13
> wait

In the dimly lit washroom, you find yourself standing before a basin, water already present, a medicinal-scented soap nearby. Two staff members, one large and silent, the other young and tense, remain expectantly nearby. The air is thick with unspoken rules. The basin, cold and hard, echoes the atmosphere. Your hands, bound, limit your movements, and your breath hitches as you wait, the silence stretching between you and the staff. The younger staff member glances at the older, who offers no words, only a stern gaze that suggests the washing is a routine procedure, not a punishment. Iven, the gentle believer, watches you with a hint of worry, but remains silent. The tension in the air is palpable, a heavy weight that presses down on you, as if the entire room is holding its breath. The water in the basin, untouched, seems to mock your inability to act.

In the dimly lit washroom, you find yourself standing before a basin, water already present, a medicinal-scented soap nearby. Two staff members, one large and silent, the other young and tense, remain expectantly nearby. The air is thick with unspoken rules. The basin, cold and hard, echoes the atmosphere. Iven, the gentle believer, watches you with a hint of worry, but remains silent. The tension in the air is palpable, a heavy weight that presses down on you, as if the entire room is holding its breath. The water in the basin, untouched, seems to mock your inability to act.

The cell, once more, becomes your confined domain. The bed beneath you offers a worn respite, the cup beside it a tantalizing promise of relief. A book rests face-down on the floor, its secrets hidden. The door, unyielding, lacks a handle on this side, while the slit at eye-level remains a silent observer.

Your eyes grow heavy, the day gradually winding toward sleep. The day's events leave you feeling more tired, your body cleaner, and your hunger eased, albeit slightly. The food arrives, yet the exchange remains unspoken. The dull tiredness behind your eyes grows more pronounced, the unease under your ribs a constant companion. Your words, though intended with clarity, come out thinner than you intend.

Nessa, the abrasive sceptic, seems content with her task of returning you to the cell and leaving you to rest. The institution's expectation of rest hangs heavy in the air, tension unresolved as the day draws

## Turn 20
> wait

In the confined, unyielding cell, you find yourself waiting, the very act mirroring the silence that envelops the space. The bed, once bleak and uninviting, seems to challenge you now, its harsh lines and cold surface a foreshadowing of the struggle ahead. Nessa, the abrasive sceptic, watches you from her corner, her eyes filled with a mix of curiosity and skepticism. The day's end approaches, Day 1 fading away, as the cell remains a constant, unyielding presence. A dull tiredness settles behind your eyes, unease gnaws at your insides, and words seem to slip through your fingers, thinner than you intend them to be. Sleep, though tempting, remains elusive.

## Turn 23
> wait

The morning air is crisp, the formal collection underway. Escorted from your cell, you find yourself in a plain corridor, the same bolts adorning the door as your cell. Hands are placed on your arms, the institution moving at its own measured pace. A sense of unease settles under your ribs as you wait, your words coming out thinner than intended. The cell remains quiet, the silence echoing down the corridor. The staff, both large and silent, practical, and younger, tense and rule-bound, continue to escort you forward. Ruan, lucid and fractured, is also present, the trio making their way toward questioning. The tension lingers, unresolved, as they expect compliance with the escort.

## Turn 24
> wait

In the institutional corridor, the rhythm of footsteps echoes, the air heavy with the weight of expectation. The large, silent staff member and the younger, tense one guide you forward, their grip firm but unyielding. Ruan, lucid and fractured, is present, too.

In the institutional corridor, the rhythm of footsteps echoes, the air heavy with the weight of expectation. The large, silent staff member and the younger, tense one guide you forward, their grip firm but unyielding. Ruan, lucid and fractured, is present, too.

Your breath hitches, the words coming out thinner than you intend. Unease sits under the ribs. You wait, the institution moving at its own pace. The cell keeps its quiet. The name on the table, a stark reminder of the journey ahead.

## Turn 25
> yes

In the sterile, brightly lit interview room, you find yourself facing Tirren, a figure of authority clad in a precisely fitted collar, as if it were an ordinary piece of clothing. The room, adorned with a table and an array of images and objects, resembles a classroom more than a place of interrogation.

The air is heavy with tension, as Tirren begins to ask questions using pictures and slow words, a method that seems aimed at drawing out answers more than conversation. A picture of water and low houses is placed before you, a question perhaps about home. Yet, your response does not meet their expectations, a palpable discomfort creeps under your ribs, and your words feel thinner than intended.

The staff members, a blend of controlled kindness and practical silence, continue to wait for your answers, the air thick with unspoken expectations. Nessa, the abrasive sceptic among them, watches you with a critical eye. The interview, it seems, is far from over.

--- J. NARRATOR OUTPUT ---
In the sterile, brightly lit interview room, you find yourself facing Tirren, a figure of authority clad in a precisely fitted collar, as if it were an ordinary piece of clothing. The room, adorned with a table and an array of images and objects, resembles a classroom more than a place of interrogation.

The air is heavy with tension, as Tirren begins to ask questions using pictures and slow words, a method that seems aimed at drawing out answers more than conversation. A picture of water and low houses is placed before you, a question perhaps about home. Yet, your response does not meet their expectations, a palpable discomfort creeps under your ribs, and your words feel thinner than intended.

The staff members, a blend of controlled kindness and practical silence, continue to wait for your answers, the air thick with unspoken expectations. Nessa, the abrasive sceptic among them, watches you with a critical eye. The interview, it seems, is far from over.

## Turn 26
> look around

In the room, the ambiance shifts, becoming brighter as if illuminated by an unseen source. A table emerges, littered with images and objects that seem to form a lesson. Opposite you sits a figure, clad in a collar that appears natural, as if it were clothing. No explanation is offered. The room is filled with three presences: Tirren, a person of controlled demeanor and precise speech; Laurie, a silent and practical figure; and Nessa, an abrasive sceptic. The air is thick with anticipation, each awaiting answers to their scheduled questions. Your words, though meant to be clear, feel thin against the silence. The images before you seem to stare back, unyielding.
